A 尚且 …, 何况 B: even A …, let alone B. 岂止: not merely (rhetorical: 岂止 A, 更是 B).		Literary; formal		ZH-HSK79-03-03-07	Chinese::HSK7-9::grammar::lit-conditions	
l!>t@%+e{K	Typecloze	Cloze Grammar::Chinese::HSK7-9::04 而 and its frames: 因…而, 为…而, 对…而言, 就…而言	这个方法简单{{c1::而::and (literary; links adjectives)}}有效。	This method is simple and effective.	Form	而: and; and yet (links two adjectives or clauses, literary). 因 A 而 B: B because of A. 为 A 而 B: B for the sake of A. 对 / 就 … 而言: as far as … is concerned (literary 对 … 来说). Form	进而: and then go further. 继而: then; subsequently (one thing after another). 故: therefore (literary 所以). 反之: conversely. 综上所述: to sum up (essay conclusions). 换言之: in other words (literary 换句话说). 简言之: in short.	进而 = a further step in the same direction; 继而 = the next event in time. Form	进而: and then go further. 继而: then; subsequently (one thing after another). 故: therefore (literary 所以). 反之: conversely. 综上所述: to sum up (essay conclusions). 换言之: in other words (literary 换句话说). 简言之: in short.	进而 = a further step in the same direction; 继而 = the next event in time. Form	No 了 for habitual past (常常去). 学会 / 听见 / 找到: the result, not just the attempt. 帮 + person (帮帮忙 is separable). 跟 … 结婚, 对 … 感兴趣. 非常 before the verb. 为 (happy for), 对 (good for). 还 + 要: another; more. 多 goes after 一个 + MW but before the MW with round numbers (一个多小时 / 十多个人). Time and place go before the verb.		Neutral		ZH-BH-05-01-01	Chinese::Beyond-HSK::grammar::errors
